## Copy & download Fluent Emoji icons

Click any icon above to open the detail panel. Four export formats are available instantly, no sign-up required.

SVG

Raw vector markup. Paste directly into HTML or React components. Scales to any size without quality loss.

JSX / TSX

React-ready component. Drop into any React or Next.js project. Works with shadcn/ui, Radix, and Tailwind CSS.

PNG

Raster export at 1×, 2× and 3× for email templates, Figma imports, app assets, and Open Graph images.

Base64

Inline data URI, embed icons in CSS background-image, email HTML, or anywhere an image URL is needed.

Works with shadcn/ui

Copy the JSX format and paste directly into any shadcn/ui component. Alternatively, installand use via `@iconify/react`, fully tree-shakeable, no icon font loading required.

## How to use Fluent Emoji icons

License SPDX

MIT

Grid Size

32px

Display Size

16px

Sample Icons

avocado, ticket, straight-ruler

Via Iconify · React

tsx

```
import { Icon } from '@iconify/react';

export default function App() {
  return <Icon icon="fluent-emoji:icon-name" width={24} />;
}
```

Via Iconify · HTML

html

```
<script src="https://code.iconify.design/iconify-icon/2.1.0/iconify-icon.min.js"></script>
<iconify-icon icon="fluent-emoji:icon-name" width="24"></iconify-icon>
```

## Frequently Asked Questions

Is Fluent Emoji free for commercial use?

Yes. Fluent Emoji is licensed under MIT, free for personal and commercial projects. [View license →](https://github.com/microsoft/fluentui-emoji/blob/main/LICENSE)

How do I download Fluent Emoji SVG icons?

Click any icon in the browser above to open the detail panel. Choose raw SVG code to copy, download as SVG or PNG, export as a React JSX component, or get a Base64 data URI, all formats free, no sign-up required.

How many icons are in Fluent Emoji?

Fluent Emoji contains 3,126 SVG icons, designed on a 32px grid.

Are Fluent Emoji icons full-color or monochrome?

Fluent Emoji icons are multi-color: they include built-in color information and do not need a fill or stroke applied. They render as-is without inheriting CSS currentColor.

What styles does Fluent Emoji offer?

Fluent Emoji includes icons in the following styles: Precise Shapes, Has Padding, Uses Stroke. You can filter by style in the browser above.

Which icon collections include Fluent Emoji?

Fluent Emoji is included in 1 curated collection: Free SVG Emoji Icons: Twemoji, Noto, Fluent Emoji & More.

Can I use Fluent Emoji with React, Next.js or shadcn/ui?

Yes. Copy the JSX export from any icon's detail panel and paste it directly into your component. For ongoing use, install `@iconify-json/fluent-emoji` and use it via `@iconify/react`, fully tree-shakeable, compatible with shadcn/ui, Tailwind CSS, Radix UI, and any React-based framework.
